| 2026-09-04T14:00Z | repo organisation | — | Root had 4,644 files / 4.4 GB with no archive. Built `ops/repo_orphans.py` (read-only; an artefact is dead only when no ledger §, board entry, backlog note, prereg, script, wrapper or queue file cites it, self-mentions excluded) and `ops/archive_orphans.py` (moves; re-scans at move time; `git mv` for tracked, manifest for every batch). Deliberately two commands so deciding and acting are never one.
